scopeFilter-round-1: [Should-fix] src/undo/preview.rs:545 in-scope — Undo of a multi-seat retirement can restore a manager pointer to the wrong seat, which is incorrect behavior in the goal-named reparent-on-retire path (B6, B7).
scopeFilter-round-1: [Should-fix] src/plan/managers/cycle_guard.rs:119 in-scope — A committed manager cycle makes /seat, who and show fail with an integrity error, which is incorrect behavior in the goal-named manager hierarchy (B5, B7, B9).
scopeFilter-round-1: [Nit] src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:70 punch-list — It only matters with an out-of-band corrupted graph, and apply fails safe without writing, so this is extra hardening and not a correctness defect in B6 reparenting.
scopeFilter-round-1: [Nit] src/plan/core_kinds.rs:796 in-scope — Retiring a teamspace reverts its default_manager rewrite, so the store, the preview and the action record disagree, which is a misleading result in the goal-named reparent path (B6).
scopeFilter-round-1: [Nit] src/observe/classify.rs:283 in-scope — A closed node-manager tab can be recorded as 'gone' instead of retired after a concurrent node-manager move, so the node-manager handling (B8, B11, B13) is not correct in that case.
scopeFilter-round-1: [Nit] CHANGELOG.md:3 punch-list — A missing changelog entry is documentation hygiene, and the goal names no behavior that depends on it.
scopeFilter-round-1: [Should-fix] evidence/hg-k4l.22/full-suite-head.log:1 punch-list — Committed test logs are repo hygiene outside every goal-named behavior; it is the only member of its cluster, so it needs no override.
cluster dropped: committed-evidence-logs — `git rm -r evidence/` (all 8 files under both hg-k4l.22 and hg-k4l.45), keep any copies under the git-ignored .superpowers/ workspace, and confirm that no other tracked *.log or home-directory path remains on the branch (`git grep -E '/var/home|alepar'` over product paths).
scope-filter: 4 in-scope · 3 punch-listed
stepBackCode-round-1: local — two duplicate pairs and spec §4.11 drift in reparent; clusters reparent-retiring-set, committed-evidence-logs
scopeFilter-round-2: [Should-fix] src/observe/mutations.rs:209 in-scope — Shows a goal-named behavior (B11 reopen of the node manager's tab after closure/move, B8 node manager never retired) is incorrect: closing the home workspace after a move retires an unrelated seat, kills live panes and relabels another teamspace's workspace.
scopeFilter-round-2: [Should-fix] src/observe/mutations.rs:242 in-scope — Shows incorrect behavior in a goal-named path: the node-manager closure branch loses the B6 reparent of default_manager on an Active teamspace, leaving the store diverged from the ClosureCascade action record.
scope-filter: 2 in-scope · 0 punch-listed

[lanes: correctness, premortem, regression] [fix-regression]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/observe/classify.rs workspace loop (~270-290) sends every non-retired seat through `seat_group(d, &seat.id, CascadeRule::Workspace, true)` with no survivors or moved_out check; the tab branch (~295-311) has one. src/observe/mutations.rs `retire_seat` (~196-210) sets `c.runtime = absent()` on every live clone before calling `node_manager_closed` (~162-176), whose `still_bound` reads `c.runtime.bound.is_some()` on the just-cleared clones, so it is always false on this path and `moved_out` is reset. `retire_teamspace` (~240) keeps the node manager's teamspace Active with an absent runtime. The planner (src/reconcile/planner.rs ~203-211, \"A rebound clone pane places its teamspace's workspace\"; the finding's `src/observe/planner.rs` path is wrong) then places the home teamspace via the still-live pane in the other workspace. Two independent FakeHerdr reproductions at 06b27d5 (alpha: foreman as node manager + another seat; beta: bob; `user_move_pane(foreman, bob_tab)`, settle, `user_close_workspace(alpha_ws)`, settle) observed `ReportWorkspaceMetadata(beta_ws, \"hg=ts_<alpha>\")`, `RenameWorkspace(beta_ws, \"alpha\")`, `CloseTab(bob's tab)`, `CreateTab(beta_ws, \"foreman ...\")`; bob ends Retired. A plain-seat control at head behaves correctly. At a12cf45 the same scenario already retired bob (from the branch's \"node manager's teamspace stays Active\" rule); hg-k4l.57/.58 added the dead `still_bound` guard, the `moved_out` clearing, the relabel and the reopen. Not on main: there the whole teamspace retires and no Active teamspace is left to place. Spec §4.9 tolerates moving the node manager (\"not reverted\"), so the starting state is supported. New tests `node_manager_closed_after_move_is_reopened` and `node_manager_moved_into_closed_tab_is_reopened` cover tab and pane closure only; none covers workspace closure after a move.\n  severity: all three seats Should-fix; narrow two-step trigger (manual cross-workspace move, then close the home workspace), but the result is a live agent killed and a seat in a different teamspace retired by a daemon with no plan involved. (regressed: iteration 1's [Nit] src/plan/core_kinds.rs:796 fix re-read the record in core_kinds.rs and in the retired branch of this function, but left this new branch with the same stale clone) [lanes: data-migrations]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/observe/mutations.rs:227 `ts = read_ts(..)` precedes the seat loop. `apply` (~340-350) puts every non-node-manager seat into `acc.retiring`; `retire_seat` -> `do_retire_seat` (src/plan/core_kinds.rs:417-430) -> `reparent` (src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:64-125) rewrites every non-retired teamspace whose `default_manager` is the retiring seat via `set_default_manager`, and the node manager's teamspace T qualifies because it stays Active; it records {T, default_manager, S->NM}. Lines 240-244 then do `let mut rec = ts.rec.clone(); rec.runtime = absent(); cx.tree.put_record(..)` with the stale pre-loop clone. `Overlay::put_record` (src/store/tree.rs:138-162) sets rev = base_rev+1 with no stale check, so `default_manager` reverts to the retired seat S. The retired branch (~252) carries the comment \"Re-read: retiring the seats may have reparented this teamspace's own default_manager.\"; this branch does not. Always reached when the node manager's workspace closes, because d848c45 routes every closure through CascadeMutation. All three seats traced source at 06b27d5; none ran it. Live resolution still works (a retired S is transparent), so the effect is store/action-record divergence on an Active teamspace plus a likely spurious \"changed since the retirement\" warning and skipped row on undo (inferred from `pointer_restores`).\n  severity: all three seats Should-fix, up from the prior Nit because the stale pointer now lands on an Active teamspace rather than an archived one.\n  fix-shape hint: Re-read the teamspace record after the seat loop in the node-manager branch, as the retired branch already does, before setting runtime absent and writing it back; add a test where the node manager's teamspace has a default_manager pointing at a sibling seat and assert the stored pointer after workspace closure."},{"section":"Escalations (need human)","position":1,"key":"src/observe/classify.rs:194","text":"src/observe/classify.rs:194 — carried from iteration 1, not re-surfaced this round, still needs the human check: UNVERIFIED external — whether a Herdr pane closes when the node manager's harness exits non-zero at once, which decides if the unconditional reopen (B11/§4.9 \"never retired, always reopened\", no throttle specified) can crash-loop close -> gone -> CreateTab/CreatePane. [lanes: correctness, data-migrations]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:~69 `retiring = BTreeSet::from([seat])` so a later-retiring seat is a valid target; src/plan/core_kinds.rs:~735 loops `do_retire_seat` with one shared `Acc`, rows appended in order (core_kinds.rs:359-361). src/undo/preview.rs:508-570 loads `Pointers` once and walks rows forward; with X.manager=N after retiring A then B (A.manager=B, B.manager=N, X.manager=A), row {X,A->B} is skipped with a false \"changed since the retirement\" warning and row {X,B->N} emits X: N->B. src/undo/compensate.rs:378-379 applies that list, so X ends at B, not A. [lanes: correctness]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/plan/managers/edit.rs:104-107,167 builds `members` excluding `Lifecycle::Retired` and passes them to `would_create_cycle`; src/plan/managers/cycle_guard.rs:119 subjects are `!s.retired && teamspaces.contains(..)`; src/model/manager.rs `effective_manager` resolves through retired seats to the teamspace `default_manager`, and `cycle_from` walks only from the given subjects. Sequence A.manager=R (A in U, R in T); retire A; retire R; resurrect A; `manager set T A` passes with an empty subject set and leaves A -> R -> T.default = A. `ancestors` then returns Integrity (\"forms a manager cycle\"). Design line 259 (\"make_plan refuses any plan whose effects would leave a manager chain cyclic\") and line 199 (cycles arise only from out-of-band edits) are contradicted. Traced from source by all three seats, not executed.\n  fix-shape hint: For a `teamspace.default_manager` edit, add as walk subjects every seat (live or retired) whose chain passes through that teamspace, or walk from the manager seat itself; alternatively have resurrect rewrite a pointer that names a retired seat."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":3,"key":"[Nit] src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:70","text":"[Nit] src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:70 — `reparent` computes the retiring seat's nearest surviving manager before checking whether any record points at that seat, so a seat with no reports but a broken manager chain plans cleanly and then fails at apply time. [lanes: correctness]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/plan/managers/reparent.rs:70 calls `pointers.nearest_surviving_manager(seat, &retiring)` unconditionally; src/model/manager.rs:203 goes through `ancestors()`, which returns `StoreError::Integrity` on a cycle or dangling pointer; `do_retire_seat` (core_kinds.rs:427) passes `acc.lenient_reparent`, true only on the observed-cascade path (observe/mutations.rs:297). `reparent_effects` (reparent.rs:~144,~160) only walks holders whose manager is in `retiring`, so the preview of a leaf seat with a corrupt chain is empty and succeeds. Design §4.11 says the preview exists \"so the apply-time diff matches\". Fail-safe: apply errors, nothing is written. Needs an out-of-band corrupted graph.\n  fix-shape hint: Compute the target lazily, only when at least one seat or teamspace pointer names the retiring seat, or make the preview walk the retiring seat's own chain as well."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":4,"key":"[Nit] src/plan/core_kinds.rs:796","text":"[Nit] src/plan/core_kinds.rs:796 — Retiring a teamspace whose `default_manager` is one of its own seats loses that teamspace's reparent rewrite: both `TeamspaceRetire::mutate` and the observed `CascadeMutation::retire_teamspace` write back a TeamspaceRecord read before the seat loop, while the action record still lists the rewrite. [lanes: correctness]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗-survived / ground ✓)\n  evidence: core_kinds.rs:~796 `ts = read_ts(..)` precedes the `do_retire_seat` loop; `reparent` rewrites every non-Retired teamspace whose default_manager is the retiring seat, T included, via `set_default_manager` (plan/managers/mod.rs:147-161); after the loop `ts.rec.clone()` with lifecycle=Retired goes through `Overlay::put_record` (store/tree.rs:~138), which sets rev=base_rev+1 with no stale check, so default_manager reverts to the retired seat. `acc.reparented` keeps the {T, default_manager, R->M} row; `reparent_effects` previews the effect. Same stale clone in observe/mutations.rs:~224. Seats agree no live pointer is wrong: the archived teamspace's pointer to a retired seat resolves transparently; the cost is preview/action-record/store disagreement and a spurious undo warning.\n  severity: two of three seats rated Nit (refute seat Should-fix); kept at Nit because the profile is a single-operator local tool and the defect only affects an archived record and record fidelity, with no live-state effect.\n  fix-shape hint: Re-read the teamspace record after the seat loop before setting lifecycle=Retired, or exclude the retiring teamspace itself from the default_manager rewrite."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":5,"key":"[Nit] src/observe/classify.rs:283","text":"[Nit] src/observe/classify.rs:283 — The observer decides at classification time, from a head read before the snapshot, whether a closed seat counts as the node manager; the apply-time re-check can only suppress a retirement, never add one, so a `node-manager set` that commits during the observer's awaited snapshot leaves the old node manager's closed tab or workspace recorded as 'gone' (Absent, still Active) instead of retired, and that is never re-derived. [lanes: concurrency-async]\n  verdict: confirmed (reproduce ✓ / refute ✗ voted REJECT as immaterial, minority / ground ✓)\n  evidence: src/observe/step.rs:489-566 calls `wait_writer_idle` once, reads `head`, loads `DesiredRuntime` at that head, then awaits `herdr.snapshot()` and `process_info` before classify and commit_ops; classify.rs node-manager branches call `node_manager_gone` and push `gone`, emitting no Cascade; `CascadeMutation` (mutations.rs:314) re-checks `live_node_manager` only for cascades; once commit_ops succeeds the baseline advances. Design §4.9 says the re-check closes \"the window between classification and a concurrent node-manager set\"; it closes it in one direction. The refute seat agrees on mechanics and rejects only on materiality (sub-second race, user-fixable with `seat retire`); the two CONFIRM seats rate it Nit for the same reasons.\n  fix-shape hint: Either re-derive the node-manager decision in `commit_ops` when head moved past the classification head, or route node-manager closures through a Cascade-like op the mutation can re-evaluate, and reword §4.9 to state the one-sided guarantee."},{"section":"Confirmed findings","position":6,"key":"[Nit] CHANGELOG.md:3","text":"[Nit] CHANGELOG.md:3 — `## Unreleased` is not updated though the branch makes user-visible changes: exit codes 5/6, removal of the daemon-free fallbacks in `who`/`ops`/`request`/`undo`, silently dropped references now failing as integrity errors, `who` gaining a second `manager:` line, a stderr warning on every command for existing graphs, and new plan kinds and templates. On apply, R is live when W retires, so `do_retire_seat` rewrites R's pointer; the preview misses that rewrite because it treats R as retired. These should time out or fail their assertions.\n  - The tier-2 twins of the same flow in daemon_composed needed exactly this opt-out to pass. This is static analysis; tier 3 was not run.\n  - Plan Task 22 lists e2e_private_herdr.rs as a file to fix and says tier 3 should run where available."},{"section":"Findings","position":2,"key":"The end-to-end integration sweep the spec and plan require has not landed. hg-k4l.22 is open, and tests/manager_seats_sweep.rs does not exist at head. None is exercised from a CLI or daemon entry point in tests/. §4.14 puts \"CLI behaviour (exit codes 2/5/6, warning line) in tests/ with TestRoot\", and exit 6 has no tests/ coverage."},{"section":"Findings","position":3,"key":"Six composed fixtures call `support::node_manager_opt_out`, which puts the graph into a dangling-node-manager state.
